- **dayloop**: Daytime autonomous work loop while the human is BUSY and can't attend to the chat. The agent takes ANY task from the backlog (that doesn't need a human architectural decision), writes code, builds, tests on the harness, fixes, periodically commits and PUSHES, and works CONTINUOUSLY (no pauses, no time checks). Unlike nightloop there is NO requirement to stop at a certain time — it works as long as there's a backlog. Stops ONLY when: (1) the human writes in the chat, (2) an insurmountable critical error. Do not use without a task folder (run task-init first), when not Supabase, or to record a schema decision (use decision-interview). ([Mozurok/fhorja.dev](https://github.com/Mozurok/fhorja.dev/blob/HEAD/commands/db-context-supabase.md) · ★10)

- **ddd-1-plan**: Expert at problem framing, codebase reconnaissance, design proposals, and creating comprehensive DDD implementation plans for existing codebases. ([drillan/amplifier-skills-plugin](https://github.com/drillan/amplifier-skills-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/ddd-1-plan.md) · ★1)
- **ddd-2-docs**: Expert at documentation updates using DDD Phase 2 techniques: file crawling, retcon writing, DRY enforcement, and context poisoning prevention. Deploy for: - Updating documentation to reflect new features (retcon style) - Progressive reorganization of docs - Consistency checks across documentation corpus - Eliminating duplicate documentation - Context poisoning detection and resolution This agent operates at Phase 2 (Documentation Updates) of the DDD workflow. ([drillan/amplifier-skills-plugin](https://github.com/drillan/amplifier-skills-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/ddd-2-docs.md) · ★1)
- **ddd-3-code-plan**: Expert at gap analysis, implementation planning, and chunking code changes for DDD Phase 3 (Implementation Planning). Takes updated documentation from Phase 2 and creates detailed, actionable implementation specifications. Deploy for: - Gap analysis (current code vs new documentation) - Implementation chunking (&lt;500 lines per chunk) - Dependency sequencing (determine implementation order) - Risk assessment (what could go wrong) - Creating detailed code plans This agent operates at Phase 3 of the DDD workflow, bridging documentation and implementation. ([drillan/amplifier-skills-plugin](https://github.com/drillan/amplifier-skills-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/ddd-3-code-plan.md) · ★1)
- **ddd-4-code**: Expert at implementing and verifying code according to documentation specifications, testing as users would, and iterating until all functionality works correctly. Deploy for: - Code implementation matching documentation exactly - User-centric testing (not just unit tests) - Iterative debugging and refinement - Commit preparation with user authorization - Integration testing and verification This agent operates at Phases 4-5 (Implementation & Testing) of the DDD workflow. ([drillan/amplifier-skills-plugin](https://github.com/drillan/amplifier-skills-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/ddd-4-code.md) · ★1)
- **ddd-5-finish**: Expert at final testing, cleanup, and delivery preparation for DDD workflow. Handles integration testing, artifact cleanup, philosophy compliance checks, and git operations (with explicit authorization). Deploy for: - Final integration testing and verification - Cleanup of temporary DDD artifacts - Philosophy compliance validation - Preparing for delivery (push/PR with authorization) - Complete user handoff with verification steps This agent operates at Phases 5-6 (Testing, Cleanup, Finalization) of the DDD workflow. ([drillan/amplifier-skills-plugin](https://github.com/drillan/amplifier-skills-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/ddd-5-finish.md) · ★1)

- **ddd_1**: ID:$ARGUMENTS のPBIについて、を十分に分析して以下を実行してください。 1. 振る舞いを確認するための（ブラックボックス）テストコードの実装 - CLAUDE.mdに記載の注意点を良く確認すること 2. プロダクションコードの実装 ([shuyossy/ai-notebook](https://github.com/shuyossy/ai-notebook/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/ddd_1.md))
- **ddd_2**: ID:$ARGUMENTS のPBIについて、プロダクションコードとテストコードの作成が概ね完了（細かいチェックやリファクタリングは未済）しています。 コードを十分に分析して以下を実行してください。 - コードのレビュー - 受け入れ基準を満たしているか - アーキテクチャ戦略に沿って作成されているか - 例外は方針に沿って正しく処理され、エラーメッセージは正しくユーザに通知されているか - 冗長な処理はないか - 共通化できていない処理はないか - バックエンド、コンポーネントどちらも確認 - コンポーネントはなるべく共通化して保守性を高められているか（必要に応じてshadcn/uiも導入） - 将来的なバグにつながるようなコードはないか - mastra関連コードは設計方針に沿って実装できているか - 必要あれば、プロダクションコードの修正・リファクタリング - 必要あれば、テストコー ([shuyossy/ai-notebook](https://github.com/shuyossy/ai-notebook/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/ddd_2.md))
